\subsection{Problem 2: The Bottleneck}\label{problem-2-the-bottleneck}

\textbf{Scenario:} Software Development. Coding takes longer than
Documentation.

\textbf{Activity Data:}

\begin{longtable}[]{@{}llll@{}}
\toprule\noalign{}
Activity & Description & Pred & Duration \\
\midrule\noalign{}
\endhead
\bottomrule\noalign{}
\endlastfoot
S & Start Specs & - & 2 \\
C & Code Module & S & 8 \\
D & Write Docs & S & 3 \\
T & Testing & C, D & 4 \\
\end{longtable}

\textbf{Mathematical Solution:}

\textbf{Forward Pass:} 1. \textbf{S:} \(0 \to 2\). 2. \textbf{C:} Starts
after S(2). \(2 + 8 = 10\). 3. \textbf{D:} Starts after S(2).
\(2 + 3 = 5\). 4. \textbf{T:} Waits for C(10) \& D(5).
\(\max(10, 5) = 10\). \(10 + 4 = 14\). \emph{Project Ends: Day 14.}

\textbf{Backward Pass:} 1. \textbf{T:} Finish by 14. Start by
\(14-4=10\). 2. \textbf{C:} Must finish by T's start (10). Start by
\(10-8=2\). 3. \textbf{D:} Must finish by T's start (10). Start by
\(10-3=7\). 4. \textbf{S:} Successors C(starts 2) \& D(starts 7).
\(\min(2, 7) = 2\). Start by \(2-2=0\).

\textbf{Float Calculation:} * \textbf{Float C:} \(LS(2) - ES(2) = 0\)
(Critical) * \textbf{Float D:} \(LS(7) - ES(2) = 5\) (Slack)

\textbf{Interpretation:} The Documentation team (D) can delay their
start by up to 5 days without affecting the launch date. The Coding team
(C) has zero wiggle room.

\textbf{Critical Path:} \(S \to C \to T\)

\begin{quote}
\textbf{Python solution}
\end{quote}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\ImportTok{import}\NormalTok{ networkx }\ImportTok{as}\NormalTok{ nx}
\ImportTok{import}\NormalTok{ pandas }\ImportTok{as}\NormalTok{ pd}

\CommentTok{\# 1. Define the Project Data (Updated for Problem 2)}
\NormalTok{tasks }\OperatorTok{=}\NormalTok{ \{}
    \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}S\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: \{}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}desc\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}Start Specs\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}duration\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: }\DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}predecessors\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: []\},}
    \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}C\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: \{}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}desc\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}Code Module\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}duration\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: }\DecValTok{8}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}predecessors\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: [}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}S\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{]\},}
    \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}D\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: \{}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}desc\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}Write Docs\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{,  }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}duration\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: }\DecValTok{3}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}predecessors\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: [}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}S\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{]\},}
    \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}T\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: \{}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}desc\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}Testing\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{,     }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}duration\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: }\DecValTok{4}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}predecessors\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: [}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}C\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}D\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{]\}}
\NormalTok{\}}

\CommentTok{\# 2. Build Graph}
\NormalTok{G }\OperatorTok{=}\NormalTok{ nx.DiGraph()}
\ControlFlowTok{for}\NormalTok{ task, data }\KeywordTok{in}\NormalTok{ tasks.items():}
\NormalTok{    G.add\_node(task, duration}\OperatorTok{=}\NormalTok{data[}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}duration\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{], desc}\OperatorTok{=}\NormalTok{data[}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}desc\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{])}
    \ControlFlowTok{for}\NormalTok{ pred }\KeywordTok{in}\NormalTok{ data[}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}predecessors\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{]:}
\NormalTok{        G.add\_edge(pred, task)}

\CommentTok{\# 3. Forward Pass (Early Start/Finish)}
\NormalTok{early\_start }\OperatorTok{=}\NormalTok{ \{\}}
\NormalTok{early\_finish }\OperatorTok{=}\NormalTok{ \{\}}
\ControlFlowTok{for}\NormalTok{ task }\KeywordTok{in}\NormalTok{ nx.topological\_sort(G):}
    \CommentTok{\# ES = Max of predecessor EF}
\NormalTok{    predecessors }\OperatorTok{=} \BuiltInTok{list}\NormalTok{(G.predecessors(task))}
\NormalTok{    es }\OperatorTok{=} \BuiltInTok{max}\NormalTok{((early\_finish[p] }\ControlFlowTok{for}\NormalTok{ p }\KeywordTok{in}\NormalTok{ predecessors), default}\OperatorTok{=}\DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{)}
    
\NormalTok{    duration }\OperatorTok{=}\NormalTok{ G.nodes[task][}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}duration\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{]}
\NormalTok{    early\_start[task] }\OperatorTok{=}\NormalTok{ es}
\NormalTok{    early\_finish[task] }\OperatorTok{=}\NormalTok{ es }\OperatorTok{+}\NormalTok{ duration}

\NormalTok{project\_duration }\OperatorTok{=} \BuiltInTok{max}\NormalTok{(early\_finish.values())}

\CommentTok{\# 4. Backward Pass (Late Start/Finish)}
\NormalTok{late\_start }\OperatorTok{=}\NormalTok{ \{\}}
\NormalTok{late\_finish }\OperatorTok{=}\NormalTok{ \{\}}
\ControlFlowTok{for}\NormalTok{ task }\KeywordTok{in} \BuiltInTok{reversed}\NormalTok{(}\BuiltInTok{list}\NormalTok{(nx.topological\_sort(G))):}
\NormalTok{    successors }\OperatorTok{=} \BuiltInTok{list}\NormalTok{(G.successors(task))}
    \CommentTok{\# LF = Min of successor LS}
\NormalTok{    lf }\OperatorTok{=} \BuiltInTok{min}\NormalTok{((late\_start[s] }\ControlFlowTok{for}\NormalTok{ s }\KeywordTok{in}\NormalTok{ successors), default}\OperatorTok{=}\NormalTok{project\_duration)}
    
\NormalTok{    duration }\OperatorTok{=}\NormalTok{ G.nodes[task][}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}duration\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{]}
\NormalTok{    late\_start[task] }\OperatorTok{=}\NormalTok{ lf }\OperatorTok{{-}}\NormalTok{ duration}
\NormalTok{    late\_finish[task] }\OperatorTok{=}\NormalTok{ lf}

\CommentTok{\# 5. Calculate Slack \& Critical Path}
\NormalTok{slack }\OperatorTok{=}\NormalTok{ \{\}}
\NormalTok{critical\_path }\OperatorTok{=}\NormalTok{ []}
\ControlFlowTok{for}\NormalTok{ task }\KeywordTok{in}\NormalTok{ tasks:}
\NormalTok{    s }\OperatorTok{=}\NormalTok{ late\_start[task] }\OperatorTok{{-}}\NormalTok{ early\_start[task]}
\NormalTok{    slack[task] }\OperatorTok{=}\NormalTok{ s}
    \ControlFlowTok{if}\NormalTok{ s }\OperatorTok{==} \DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{:}
\NormalTok{        critical\_path.append(task)}

\CommentTok{\# 6. Display Results}
\NormalTok{df }\OperatorTok{=}\NormalTok{ pd.DataFrame(\{}
    \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}Description\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: [tasks[t][}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}desc\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{] }\ControlFlowTok{for}\NormalTok{ t }\KeywordTok{in}\NormalTok{ tasks],}
    \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}Duration\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: [tasks[t][}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}duration\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{] }\ControlFlowTok{for}\NormalTok{ t }\KeywordTok{in}\NormalTok{ tasks],}
    \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}ES\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: [early\_start[t] }\ControlFlowTok{for}\NormalTok{ t }\KeywordTok{in}\NormalTok{ tasks],}
    \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}EF\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: [early\_finish[t] }\ControlFlowTok{for}\NormalTok{ t }\KeywordTok{in}\NormalTok{ tasks],}
    \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}LS\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: [late\_start[t] }\ControlFlowTok{for}\NormalTok{ t }\KeywordTok{in}\NormalTok{ tasks],}
    \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}LF\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: [late\_finish[t] }\ControlFlowTok{for}\NormalTok{ t }\KeywordTok{in}\NormalTok{ tasks],}
    \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}Slack\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: [slack[t] }\ControlFlowTok{for}\NormalTok{ t }\KeywordTok{in}\NormalTok{ tasks],}
    \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}Critical\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: [}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}Yes\textquotesingle{}} \ControlFlowTok{if}\NormalTok{ s }\OperatorTok{==} \DecValTok{0} \ControlFlowTok{else} \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}No\textquotesingle{}} \ControlFlowTok{for}\NormalTok{ s }\KeywordTok{in}\NormalTok{ slack.values()]}
\NormalTok{\}, index}\OperatorTok{=}\NormalTok{tasks.keys())}

\BuiltInTok{print}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"{-}{-}{-} CPM Schedule Analysis: The Bottleneck {-}{-}{-}"}\NormalTok{)}
\BuiltInTok{print}\NormalTok{(df[[}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}Description\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}Duration\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}ES\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}EF\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}Slack\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}Critical\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{]])}
\BuiltInTok{print}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"{-}"} \OperatorTok{*} \DecValTok{60}\NormalTok{)}
\BuiltInTok{print}\NormalTok{(}\SpecialStringTok{f"Total Project Duration: }\SpecialCharTok{\{}\NormalTok{project\_duration}\SpecialCharTok{\}}\SpecialStringTok{ Days"}\NormalTok{)}
\BuiltInTok{print}\NormalTok{(}\SpecialStringTok{f"Critical Path: }\SpecialCharTok{\{}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{} {-}\textgreater{} \textquotesingle{}}\SpecialCharTok{.}\NormalTok{join(critical\_path)}\SpecialCharTok{\}}\SpecialStringTok{"}\NormalTok{)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

   Description  Duration  ES  EF  Slack Critical
S  Start Specs         2   0   2      0      Yes
C  Code Module         8   2  10      0      Yes
D   Write Docs         3   2   5      5       No
T      Testing         4  10  14      0      Yes

\subsection{Problem 3: The ``Merge''
Challenge}\label{problem-3-the-merge-challenge}

\textbf{Scenario:} Construction. Notice how Activity F depends on three
things.

\textbf{Activity Data:}

\begin{longtable}[]{@{}lll@{}}
\toprule\noalign{}
Act & Pred & Time \\
\midrule\noalign{}
\endhead
\bottomrule\noalign{}
\endlastfoot
A & - & 5 \\
B & A & 3 \\
C & A & 4 \\
D & A & 6 \\
E & B & 2 \\
F & C, D, E & 3 \\
\end{longtable}

\textbf{Mathematical Solution:}

\textbf{Step 1: The Merge (Node F)} * First, compute B, C, D (all start
after A finishes at 5).

\begin{verbatim}
- **B:** $5 + 3 = 8$. (E follows B: $8 + 2 = 10$).
- **C:** $5 + 4 = 9$.
- **D:** $5 + 6 = 11$.
\end{verbatim}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \textbf{F} depends on C(9), D(11), E(10).
\item
  \textbf{F's Start:} \(\max(9, 11, 10) = 11\).
\item
  \textbf{F's Finish:} \(11 + 3 = 14\).
\end{itemize}

\textbf{Step 2: Backward from Merge}

\begin{itemize}
\item
  \textbf{F:} \(LS = 11\).
\item
  Now F pushes back on C, D, and E. They ALL must finish by 11.

  \begin{itemize}
  \tightlist
  \item
    \textbf{D:} \(LF=11\). Duration 6. \(LS = 5\).
  \item
    \textbf{C:} \(LF=11\). Duration 4. \(LS = 7\).
  \item
    \textbf{E:} \(LF=11\). Duration 2. \(LS = 9\). (This pushes back on
    B).
  \end{itemize}
\end{itemize}

\textbf{Float Analysis:}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \textbf{Path A-D-F:} \(5+6+3=14\). (Length 14). Float = 0.
\item
  \textbf{Path A-C-F:} \(5+4+3=12\). (Length 12). Float = 2.
\item
  \textbf{Path A-B-E-F:} \(5+3+2+3=13\). (Length 13). Float = 1.
\end{itemize}

\textbf{Critical Path:} \(A \to D \to F\)

\begin{quote}
\textbf{Python solution}
\end{quote}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\ImportTok{import}\NormalTok{ networkx }\ImportTok{as}\NormalTok{ nx}
\ImportTok{import}\NormalTok{ pandas }\ImportTok{as}\NormalTok{ pd}

\CommentTok{\# 1. Define the Project Data (Problem 3: The Merge)}
\NormalTok{tasks }\OperatorTok{=}\NormalTok{ \{}
    \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}A\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: \{}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}duration\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: }\DecValTok{5}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}predecessors\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: []\},}
    \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}B\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: \{}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}duration\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: }\DecValTok{3}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}predecessors\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: [}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}A\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{]\},}
    \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}C\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: \{}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}duration\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: }\DecValTok{4}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}predecessors\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: [}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}A\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{]\},}
    \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}D\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: \{}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}duration\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: }\DecValTok{6}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}predecessors\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: [}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}A\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{]\},}
    \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}E\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: \{}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}duration\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: }\DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}predecessors\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: [}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}B\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{]\},}
    \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}F\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: \{}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}duration\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: }\DecValTok{3}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}predecessors\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: [}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}C\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}D\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}E\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{]\}}
\NormalTok{\}}

\CommentTok{\# 2. Build Graph}
\NormalTok{G }\OperatorTok{=}\NormalTok{ nx.DiGraph()}
\ControlFlowTok{for}\NormalTok{ task, data }\KeywordTok{in}\NormalTok{ tasks.items():}
\NormalTok{    G.add\_node(task, duration}\OperatorTok{=}\NormalTok{data[}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}duration\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{])}
    \ControlFlowTok{for}\NormalTok{ pred }\KeywordTok{in}\NormalTok{ data[}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}predecessors\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{]:}
\NormalTok{        G.add\_edge(pred, task)}

\CommentTok{\# 3. Forward Pass (Early Start/Finish)}
\NormalTok{early\_start }\OperatorTok{=}\NormalTok{ \{\}}
\NormalTok{early\_finish }\OperatorTok{=}\NormalTok{ \{\}}
\CommentTok{\# Topological sort ensures we process parents before children}
\ControlFlowTok{for}\NormalTok{ task }\KeywordTok{in}\NormalTok{ nx.topological\_sort(G):}
\NormalTok{    predecessors }\OperatorTok{=} \BuiltInTok{list}\NormalTok{(G.predecessors(task))}
    \CommentTok{\# ES is Max of predecessors\textquotesingle{} EF}
\NormalTok{    es }\OperatorTok{=} \BuiltInTok{max}\NormalTok{((early\_finish[p] }\ControlFlowTok{for}\NormalTok{ p }\KeywordTok{in}\NormalTok{ predecessors), default}\OperatorTok{=}\DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{)}
    
\NormalTok{    duration }\OperatorTok{=}\NormalTok{ G.nodes[task][}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}duration\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{]}
\NormalTok{    early\_start[task] }\OperatorTok{=}\NormalTok{ es}
\NormalTok{    early\_finish[task] }\OperatorTok{=}\NormalTok{ es }\OperatorTok{+}\NormalTok{ duration}

\NormalTok{project\_duration }\OperatorTok{=} \BuiltInTok{max}\NormalTok{(early\_finish.values())}

\CommentTok{\# 4. Backward Pass (Late Start/Finish)}
\NormalTok{late\_start }\OperatorTok{=}\NormalTok{ \{\}}
\NormalTok{late\_finish }\OperatorTok{=}\NormalTok{ \{\}}
\ControlFlowTok{for}\NormalTok{ task }\KeywordTok{in} \BuiltInTok{reversed}\NormalTok{(}\BuiltInTok{list}\NormalTok{(nx.topological\_sort(G))):}
\NormalTok{    successors }\OperatorTok{=} \BuiltInTok{list}\NormalTok{(G.successors(task))}
    \CommentTok{\# LF is Min of successors\textquotesingle{} LS}
\NormalTok{    lf }\OperatorTok{=} \BuiltInTok{min}\NormalTok{((late\_start[s] }\ControlFlowTok{for}\NormalTok{ s }\KeywordTok{in}\NormalTok{ successors), default}\OperatorTok{=}\NormalTok{project\_duration)}
    
\NormalTok{    duration }\OperatorTok{=}\NormalTok{ G.nodes[task][}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}duration\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{]}
\NormalTok{    late\_start[task] }\OperatorTok{=}\NormalTok{ lf }\OperatorTok{{-}}\NormalTok{ duration}
\NormalTok{    late\_finish[task] }\OperatorTok{=}\NormalTok{ lf}

\CommentTok{\# 5. Calculate Slack \& Critical Path}
\NormalTok{slack }\OperatorTok{=}\NormalTok{ \{\}}
\NormalTok{critical\_path }\OperatorTok{=}\NormalTok{ []}
\ControlFlowTok{for}\NormalTok{ task }\KeywordTok{in}\NormalTok{ tasks:}
\NormalTok{    s }\OperatorTok{=}\NormalTok{ late\_start[task] }\OperatorTok{{-}}\NormalTok{ early\_start[task]}
\NormalTok{    slack[task] }\OperatorTok{=}\NormalTok{ s}
    \ControlFlowTok{if}\NormalTok{ s }\OperatorTok{==} \DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{:}
\NormalTok{        critical\_path.append(task)}

\CommentTok{\# 6. Display Results}
\NormalTok{df }\OperatorTok{=}\NormalTok{ pd.DataFrame(\{}
    \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}Duration\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: [tasks[t][}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}duration\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{] }\ControlFlowTok{for}\NormalTok{ t }\KeywordTok{in}\NormalTok{ tasks],}
    \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}ES\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: [early\_start[t] }\ControlFlowTok{for}\NormalTok{ t }\KeywordTok{in}\NormalTok{ tasks],}
    \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}EF\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: [early\_finish[t] }\ControlFlowTok{for}\NormalTok{ t }\KeywordTok{in}\NormalTok{ tasks],}
    \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}LS\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: [late\_start[t] }\ControlFlowTok{for}\NormalTok{ t }\KeywordTok{in}\NormalTok{ tasks],}
    \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}LF\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: [late\_finish[t] }\ControlFlowTok{for}\NormalTok{ t }\KeywordTok{in}\NormalTok{ tasks],}
    \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}Slack\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: [slack[t] }\ControlFlowTok{for}\NormalTok{ t }\KeywordTok{in}\NormalTok{ tasks],}
    \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}Critical\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: [}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}Yes\textquotesingle{}} \ControlFlowTok{if}\NormalTok{ s }\OperatorTok{==} \DecValTok{0} \ControlFlowTok{else} \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}No\textquotesingle{}} \ControlFlowTok{for}\NormalTok{ s }\KeywordTok{in}\NormalTok{ slack.values()]}
\NormalTok{\}, index}\OperatorTok{=}\NormalTok{tasks.keys())}

\BuiltInTok{print}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"{-}{-}{-} CPM Schedule: The Merge Challenge {-}{-}{-}"}\NormalTok{)}
\BuiltInTok{print}\NormalTok{(df)}
\BuiltInTok{print}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"{-}"} \OperatorTok{*} \DecValTok{60}\NormalTok{)}
\BuiltInTok{print}\NormalTok{(}\SpecialStringTok{f"Total Project Duration: }\SpecialCharTok{\{}\NormalTok{project\_duration}\SpecialCharTok{\}}\SpecialStringTok{ Days"}\NormalTok{)}
\BuiltInTok{print}\NormalTok{(}\SpecialStringTok{f"Critical Path: }\SpecialCharTok{\{}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{} {-}\textgreater{} \textquotesingle{}}\SpecialCharTok{.}\NormalTok{join(critical\_path)}\SpecialCharTok{\}}\SpecialStringTok{"}\NormalTok{)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{verbatim}

   Duration  ES  EF  LS  LF  Slack Critical
A         5   0   5   0   5      0      Yes
B         3   5   8   6   9      1       No
C         4   5   9   7  11      2       No
D         6   5  11   5  11      0      Yes

\subsection{Problem 4: Complex
Dependencies}\label{problem-4-complex-dependencies}

\textbf{Scenario:} A tech product launch.

\begin{longtable}[]{@{}llll@{}}
\toprule\noalign{}
Act & Desc & Pred & Time \\
\midrule\noalign{}
\endhead
\bottomrule\noalign{}
\endlastfoot
A & Market Research & - & 10 \\
B & Product Design & A & 20 \\
C & Ad Campaign & A & 15 \\
D & Prototype & B & 10 \\
E & Manufacture & D & 30 \\
F & Launch Event & C, E & 5 \\
\end{longtable}

\textbf{Mathematical Solution:}

\textbf{Trace Table:}

\begin{longtable}[]{@{}llllllll@{}}
\toprule\noalign{}
Node & Pred & ES & EF (=ES+Dur) & Succ & LF & LS (=LF-Dur) & Float \\
\midrule\noalign{}
\endhead
\bottomrule\noalign{}
\endlastfoot
\textbf{A} & - & 0 & 10 & B, C & 10 & 0 & 0 \\
\textbf{B} & A & 10 & 30 & D & 30 & 10 & 0 \\
\textbf{C} & A & 10 & 25 & F & 70 & 55 & \textbf{45} \\
\textbf{D} & B & 30 & 40 & E & 40 & 30 & 0 \\
\textbf{E} & D & 40 & 70 & F & 70 & 40 & 0 \\
\textbf{F} & C, E & \textbf{70} & 75 & - & 75 & 70 & 0 \\
\end{longtable}

\textbf{Calculations to Watch:}

\begin{itemize}
\item
  For \textbf{F}: Predecessors C(25) and E(70). \(\max(25, 70) = 70\).
\item
  For \textbf{C}: Successor F requires start at 70. Duration 15.
  \(LS = 70-15 = 55\).

  \begin{itemize}
  \tightlist
  \item
    Float for C: \(55 - 10 = 45\).
  \item
    \emph{Insight:} The Ad Campaign (C) is wildly non-critical. You have
    45 days of slack!
  \end{itemize}
\end{itemize}

\textbf{Critical Path:} \(A \to B \to D \to E \to F\)

\begin{quote}
\textbf{Python solution}
\end{quote}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\ImportTok{import}\NormalTok{ networkx }\ImportTok{as}\NormalTok{ nx}
\ImportTok{import}\NormalTok{ pandas }\ImportTok{as}\NormalTok{ pd}

\CommentTok{\# 1. Define the Project Data (Problem 4: Tech Launch)}
\NormalTok{tasks }\OperatorTok{=}\NormalTok{ \{}
    \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}A\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: \{}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}desc\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}Market Research\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}duration\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: }\DecValTok{10}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}predecessors\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: []\},}
    \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}B\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: \{}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}desc\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}Product Design\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{,  }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}duration\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: }\DecValTok{20}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}predecessors\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: [}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}A\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{]\},}
    \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}C\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: \{}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}desc\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}Ad Campaign\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{,     }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}duration\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: }\DecValTok{15}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}predecessors\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: [}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}A\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{]\},}
    \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}D\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: \{}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}desc\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}Prototype\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{,       }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}duration\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: }\DecValTok{10}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}predecessors\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: [}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}B\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{]\},}
    \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}E\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: \{}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}desc\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}Manufacture\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{,     }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}duration\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: }\DecValTok{30}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}predecessors\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: [}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}D\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{]\},}
    \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}F\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: \{}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}desc\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}Launch Event\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{,    }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}duration\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: }\DecValTok{5}\NormalTok{,  }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}predecessors\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: [}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}C\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}E\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{]\}}
\NormalTok{\}}

\CommentTok{\# 2. Build Graph}
\NormalTok{G }\OperatorTok{=}\NormalTok{ nx.DiGraph()}
\ControlFlowTok{for}\NormalTok{ task, data }\KeywordTok{in}\NormalTok{ tasks.items():}
\NormalTok{    G.add\_node(task, duration}\OperatorTok{=}\NormalTok{data[}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}duration\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{], desc}\OperatorTok{=}\NormalTok{data[}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}desc\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{])}
    \ControlFlowTok{for}\NormalTok{ pred }\KeywordTok{in}\NormalTok{ data[}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}predecessors\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{]:}
\NormalTok{        G.add\_edge(pred, task)}

\CommentTok{\# 3. Forward Pass (Early Start/Finish)}
\NormalTok{early\_start }\OperatorTok{=}\NormalTok{ \{\}}
\NormalTok{early\_finish }\OperatorTok{=}\NormalTok{ \{\}}
\ControlFlowTok{for}\NormalTok{ task }\KeywordTok{in}\NormalTok{ nx.topological\_sort(G):}
\NormalTok{    predecessors }\OperatorTok{=} \BuiltInTok{list}\NormalTok{(G.predecessors(task))}
\NormalTok{    es }\OperatorTok{=} \BuiltInTok{max}\NormalTok{((early\_finish[p] }\ControlFlowTok{for}\NormalTok{ p }\KeywordTok{in}\NormalTok{ predecessors), default}\OperatorTok{=}\DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{)}
    
\NormalTok{    duration }\OperatorTok{=}\NormalTok{ G.nodes[task][}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}duration\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{]}
\NormalTok{    early\_start[task] }\OperatorTok{=}\NormalTok{ es}
\NormalTok{    early\_finish[task] }\OperatorTok{=}\NormalTok{ es }\OperatorTok{+}\NormalTok{ duration}

\NormalTok{project\_duration }\OperatorTok{=} \BuiltInTok{max}\NormalTok{(early\_finish.values())}

\CommentTok{\# 4. Backward Pass (Late Start/Finish)}
\NormalTok{late\_start }\OperatorTok{=}\NormalTok{ \{\}}
\NormalTok{late\_finish }\OperatorTok{=}\NormalTok{ \{\}}
\ControlFlowTok{for}\NormalTok{ task }\KeywordTok{in} \BuiltInTok{reversed}\NormalTok{(}\BuiltInTok{list}\NormalTok{(nx.topological\_sort(G))):}
\NormalTok{    successors }\OperatorTok{=} \BuiltInTok{list}\NormalTok{(G.successors(task))}
\NormalTok{    lf }\OperatorTok{=} \BuiltInTok{min}\NormalTok{((late\_start[s] }\ControlFlowTok{for}\NormalTok{ s }\KeywordTok{in}\NormalTok{ successors), default}\OperatorTok{=}\NormalTok{project\_duration)}
    
\NormalTok{    duration }\OperatorTok{=}\NormalTok{ G.nodes[task][}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}duration\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{]}
\NormalTok{    late\_start[task] }\OperatorTok{=}\NormalTok{ lf }\OperatorTok{{-}}\NormalTok{ duration}
\NormalTok{    late\_finish[task] }\OperatorTok{=}\NormalTok{ lf}

\CommentTok{\# 5. Calculate Slack \& Critical Path}
\NormalTok{slack }\OperatorTok{=}\NormalTok{ \{\}}
\NormalTok{critical\_path }\OperatorTok{=}\NormalTok{ []}
\ControlFlowTok{for}\NormalTok{ task }\KeywordTok{in}\NormalTok{ tasks:}
\NormalTok{    s }\OperatorTok{=}\NormalTok{ late\_start[task] }\OperatorTok{{-}}\NormalTok{ early\_start[task]}
\NormalTok{    slack[task] }\OperatorTok{=}\NormalTok{ s}
    \ControlFlowTok{if}\NormalTok{ s }\OperatorTok{==} \DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{:}
\NormalTok{        critical\_path.append(task)}

\CommentTok{\# 6. Display Results}
\NormalTok{df }\OperatorTok{=}\NormalTok{ pd.DataFrame(\{}
    \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}Description\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: [tasks[t][}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}desc\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{] }\ControlFlowTok{for}\NormalTok{ t }\KeywordTok{in}\NormalTok{ tasks],}
    \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}Duration\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: [tasks[t][}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}duration\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{] }\ControlFlowTok{for}\NormalTok{ t }\KeywordTok{in}\NormalTok{ tasks],}
    \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}ES\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: [early\_start[t] }\ControlFlowTok{for}\NormalTok{ t }\KeywordTok{in}\NormalTok{ tasks],}
    \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}EF\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: [early\_finish[t] }\ControlFlowTok{for}\NormalTok{ t }\KeywordTok{in}\NormalTok{ tasks],}
    \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}LS\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: [late\_start[t] }\ControlFlowTok{for}\NormalTok{ t }\KeywordTok{in}\NormalTok{ tasks],}
    \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}LF\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: [late\_finish[t] }\ControlFlowTok{for}\NormalTok{ t }\KeywordTok{in}\NormalTok{ tasks],}
    \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}Slack\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: [slack[t] }\ControlFlowTok{for}\NormalTok{ t }\KeywordTok{in}\NormalTok{ tasks],}
    \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}Critical\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: [}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}Yes\textquotesingle{}} \ControlFlowTok{if}\NormalTok{ s }\OperatorTok{==} \DecValTok{0} \ControlFlowTok{else} \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}No\textquotesingle{}} \ControlFlowTok{for}\NormalTok{ s }\KeywordTok{in}\NormalTok{ slack.values()]}
\NormalTok{\}, index}\OperatorTok{=}\NormalTok{tasks.keys())}

\BuiltInTok{print}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"{-}{-}{-} CPM Analysis: Tech Product Launch {-}{-}{-}"}\NormalTok{)}
\BuiltInTok{print}\NormalTok{(df[[}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}Description\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}Duration\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}ES\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}EF\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}Slack\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}Critical\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{]])}
\BuiltInTok{print}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"{-}"} \OperatorTok{*} \DecValTok{65}\NormalTok{)}
\BuiltInTok{print}\NormalTok{(}\SpecialStringTok{f"Total Project Duration: }\SpecialCharTok{\{}\NormalTok{project\_duration}\SpecialCharTok{\}}\SpecialStringTok{ Days"}\NormalTok{)}
\BuiltInTok{print}\NormalTok{(}\SpecialStringTok{f"Critical Path: }\SpecialCharTok{\{}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{} {-}\textgreater{} \textquotesingle{}}\SpecialCharTok{.}\NormalTok{join(critical\_path)}\SpecialCharTok{\}}\SpecialStringTok{"}\NormalTok{)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{verbatim}
--- CPM Analysis: Tech Product Launch ---
       Description  Duration  ES  EF  Slack Critical
A  Market Research        10   0  10      0      Yes
B   Product Design        20  10  30      0      Yes
C      Ad Campaign        15  10  25     45       No
D        Prototype        10  30  40      0      Yes
E      Manufacture        30  40  70      0      Yes
F     Launch Event         5  70  75      0      Yes
-----------------------------------------------------------------
Total Project Duration: 75 Days
Critical Path: A -> B -> D -> E -> F
\end{verbatim}

\subsection{Problem 5: The ``Exam Style'' Tabular
Problem}\label{problem-5-the-exam-style-tabular-problem}

\textbf{Scenario:} Complete the table to find the project parameters.

\textbf{Given Data:}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  A (4), B (3) start at 0.
\item
  C (2) depends on A.
\item
  D (5) depends on A.
\item
  E (6) depends on B.
\item
  F (3) depends on C, D, E.
\end{itemize}

\textbf{Mathematical Solution:}

\textbf{Forward Pass:}

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\tightlist
\item
  \textbf{A:} \(0 \to 4\).
\item
  \textbf{B:} \(0 \to 3\).
\item
  \textbf{C:} After A(4). \(4 \to 6\).
\item
  \textbf{D:} After A(4). \(4 \to 9\).
\item
  \textbf{E:} After B(3). \(3 \to 9\).
\item
  \textbf{F:} After C(6), D(9), E(9). \(\max(6, 9, 9) = 9\).
  \(9 \to 12\).
\end{enumerate}

\textbf{Backward Pass:}

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\tightlist
\item
  \textbf{F:} End 12. Start 9.
\item
  \textbf{C:} Need by 9. End 9. Start \(9-2=7\).
\item
  \textbf{D:} Need by 9. End 9. Start \(9-5=4\).
\item
  \textbf{E:} Need by 9. End 9. Start \(9-6=3\).
\item
  \textbf{A:} Succ C(start 7), D(start 4). \(\min(7, 4) = 4\). End 4.
  Start 0.
\item
  \textbf{B:} Succ E(start 3). End 3. Start 0.
\end{enumerate}

\textbf{Results Table:}

\begin{longtable}[]{@{}lllllll@{}}
\toprule\noalign{}
Activity & ES & EF & LS & LF & Float & Critical? \\
\midrule\noalign{}
\endhead
\bottomrule\noalign{}
\endlastfoot
A & 0 & 4 & 0 & 4 & 0 & \textbf{YES} \\
B & 0 & 3 & 0 & 3 & 0 & \textbf{YES} \\
C & 4 & 6 & 7 & 9 & 3 & No \\
D & 4 & 9 & 4 & 9 & 0 & \textbf{YES} \\
E & 3 & 9 & 3 & 9 & 0 & \textbf{YES} \\
F & 9 & 12 & 9 & 12 & 0 & \textbf{YES} \\
\end{longtable}

\textbf{Observation:}

This project has \textbf{Two Critical Paths}:

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\tightlist
\item
  \(A \to D \to F\) (Length \(4+5+3 = 12\))
\item
  \(B \to E \to F\) (Length \(3+6+3 = 12\))
\end{enumerate}

Both paths are bottlenecks. Delays in either A OR B will delay the
project.

\textbf{Python solution}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\ImportTok{import}\NormalTok{ networkx }\ImportTok{as}\NormalTok{ nx}
\ImportTok{import}\NormalTok{ pandas }\ImportTok{as}\NormalTok{ pd}

\CommentTok{\# 1. Define the Project Data (Problem 5)}
\NormalTok{tasks }\OperatorTok{=}\NormalTok{ \{}
    \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}A\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: \{}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}duration\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: }\DecValTok{4}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}predecessors\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: []\},}
    \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}B\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: \{}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}duration\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: }\DecValTok{3}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}predecessors\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: []\},}
    \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}C\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: \{}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}duration\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: }\DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}predecessors\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: [}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}A\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{]\},}
    \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}D\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: \{}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}duration\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: }\DecValTok{5}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}predecessors\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: [}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}A\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{]\},}
    \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}E\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: \{}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}duration\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: }\DecValTok{6}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}predecessors\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: [}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}B\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{]\},}
    \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}F\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: \{}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}duration\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: }\DecValTok{3}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}predecessors\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: [}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}C\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}D\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}E\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{]\}}
\NormalTok{\}}

\CommentTok{\# 2. Build Graph}
\NormalTok{G }\OperatorTok{=}\NormalTok{ nx.DiGraph()}
\ControlFlowTok{for}\NormalTok{ task, data }\KeywordTok{in}\NormalTok{ tasks.items():}
\NormalTok{    G.add\_node(task, duration}\OperatorTok{=}\NormalTok{data[}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}duration\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{])}
    \ControlFlowTok{for}\NormalTok{ pred }\KeywordTok{in}\NormalTok{ data[}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}predecessors\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{]:}
\NormalTok{        G.add\_edge(pred, task)}

\CommentTok{\# 3. Forward Pass (Early Start/Finish)}
\NormalTok{early\_start }\OperatorTok{=}\NormalTok{ \{\}}
\NormalTok{early\_finish }\OperatorTok{=}\NormalTok{ \{\}}
\ControlFlowTok{for}\NormalTok{ task }\KeywordTok{in}\NormalTok{ nx.topological\_sort(G):}
\NormalTok{    predecessors }\OperatorTok{=} \BuiltInTok{list}\NormalTok{(G.predecessors(task))}
    \CommentTok{\# If no preds, start at 0. Else max of preds\textquotesingle{} finish.}
\NormalTok{    es }\OperatorTok{=} \BuiltInTok{max}\NormalTok{((early\_finish[p] }\ControlFlowTok{for}\NormalTok{ p }\KeywordTok{in}\NormalTok{ predecessors), default}\OperatorTok{=}\DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{)}
    
\NormalTok{    duration }\OperatorTok{=}\NormalTok{ G.nodes[task][}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}duration\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{]}
\NormalTok{    early\_start[task] }\OperatorTok{=}\NormalTok{ es}
\NormalTok{    early\_finish[task] }\OperatorTok{=}\NormalTok{ es }\OperatorTok{+}\NormalTok{ duration}

\NormalTok{project\_duration }\OperatorTok{=} \BuiltInTok{max}\NormalTok{(early\_finish.values())}

\CommentTok{\# 4. Backward Pass (Late Start/Finish)}
\NormalTok{late\_start }\OperatorTok{=}\NormalTok{ \{\}}
\NormalTok{late\_finish }\OperatorTok{=}\NormalTok{ \{\}}
\ControlFlowTok{for}\NormalTok{ task }\KeywordTok{in} \BuiltInTok{reversed}\NormalTok{(}\BuiltInTok{list}\NormalTok{(nx.topological\_sort(G))):}
\NormalTok{    successors }\OperatorTok{=} \BuiltInTok{list}\NormalTok{(G.successors(task))}
    \CommentTok{\# If no successors, finish at Project Duration. Else min of succs\textquotesingle{} start.}
\NormalTok{    lf }\OperatorTok{=} \BuiltInTok{min}\NormalTok{((late\_start[s] }\ControlFlowTok{for}\NormalTok{ s }\KeywordTok{in}\NormalTok{ successors), default}\OperatorTok{=}\NormalTok{project\_duration)}
    
\NormalTok{    duration }\OperatorTok{=}\NormalTok{ G.nodes[task][}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}duration\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{]}
\NormalTok{    late\_start[task] }\OperatorTok{=}\NormalTok{ lf }\OperatorTok{{-}}\NormalTok{ duration}
\NormalTok{    late\_finish[task] }\OperatorTok{=}\NormalTok{ lf}

\CommentTok{\# 5. Calculate Slack \& Identify Critical Paths}
\NormalTok{slack }\OperatorTok{=}\NormalTok{ \{\}}
\NormalTok{critical\_path\_tasks }\OperatorTok{=}\NormalTok{ []}
\ControlFlowTok{for}\NormalTok{ task }\KeywordTok{in}\NormalTok{ tasks:}
\NormalTok{    s }\OperatorTok{=}\NormalTok{ late\_start[task] }\OperatorTok{{-}}\NormalTok{ early\_start[task]}
\NormalTok{    slack[task] }\OperatorTok{=}\NormalTok{ s}
    \ControlFlowTok{if}\NormalTok{ s }\OperatorTok{==} \DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{:}
\NormalTok{        critical\_path\_tasks.append(task)}

\CommentTok{\# 6. Display Results}
\NormalTok{df }\OperatorTok{=}\NormalTok{ pd.DataFrame(\{}
    \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}Duration\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: [tasks[t][}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}duration\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{] }\ControlFlowTok{for}\NormalTok{ t }\KeywordTok{in}\NormalTok{ tasks],}
    \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}ES\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: [early\_start[t] }\ControlFlowTok{for}\NormalTok{ t }\KeywordTok{in}\NormalTok{ tasks],}
    \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}EF\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: [early\_finish[t] }\ControlFlowTok{for}\NormalTok{ t }\KeywordTok{in}\NormalTok{ tasks],}
    \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}LS\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: [late\_start[t] }\ControlFlowTok{for}\NormalTok{ t }\KeywordTok{in}\NormalTok{ tasks],}
    \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}LF\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: [late\_finish[t] }\ControlFlowTok{for}\NormalTok{ t }\KeywordTok{in}\NormalTok{ tasks],}
    \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}Slack\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: [slack[t] }\ControlFlowTok{for}\NormalTok{ t }\KeywordTok{in}\NormalTok{ tasks],}
    \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}Critical\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: [}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}Yes\textquotesingle{}} \ControlFlowTok{if}\NormalTok{ s }\OperatorTok{==} \DecValTok{0} \ControlFlowTok{else} \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}No\textquotesingle{}} \ControlFlowTok{for}\NormalTok{ s }\KeywordTok{in}\NormalTok{ slack.values()]}
\NormalTok{\}, index}\OperatorTok{=}\NormalTok{tasks.keys())}

\BuiltInTok{print}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"{-}{-}{-} CPM Tabular Solution {-}{-}{-}"}\NormalTok{)}
\BuiltInTok{print}\NormalTok{(df)}
\BuiltInTok{print}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"{-}"} \OperatorTok{*} \DecValTok{60}\NormalTok{)}
\BuiltInTok{print}\NormalTok{(}\SpecialStringTok{f"Total Project Duration: }\SpecialCharTok{\{}\NormalTok{project\_duration}\SpecialCharTok{\}}\SpecialStringTok{ Days"}\NormalTok{)}
\BuiltInTok{print}\NormalTok{(}\SpecialStringTok{f"Critical Tasks: }\SpecialCharTok{\{}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}, \textquotesingle{}}\SpecialCharTok{.}\NormalTok{join(critical\_path\_tasks)}\SpecialCharTok{\}}\SpecialStringTok{"}\NormalTok{)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{verbatim}
--- CPM Tabular Solution ---
   Duration  ES  EF  LS  LF  Slack Critical
A         4   0   4   0   4      0      Yes
B         3   0   3   0   3      0      Yes
C         2   4   6   7   9      3       No
D         5   4   9   4   9      0      Yes
E         6   3   9   3   9      0      Yes
F         3   9  12   9  12      0      Yes
------------------------------------------------------------
Total Project Duration: 12 Days
Critical Tasks: A, B, D, E, F

\subsection{Sample Problem}\label{sample-problem-8}

\textbf{Problem Statement:} Solve the library construction problem from
Section 3.1 using CPM.

\textbf{Solution:}

\textbf{Forward Pass:}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  A: ES=0, EF=2
\item
  B: ES=2, EF=6
\item
  C: ES=6, EF=12
\item
  D: ES=12, EF=15
\item
  E: ES=12, EF=15
\item
  F: ES=15, EF=19
\item
  G: ES=12, EF=14
\item
  H: ES=19, EF=20
\end{itemize}

\textbf{Backward Pass:}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  H: LF=20, LS=19
\item
  F: LF=19, LS=15
\item
  G: LF=19, LS=17
\item
  D: LF=15, LS=12
\item
  E: LF=15, LS=12
\item
  C: LF=12, LS=6
\item
  B: LF=6, LS=2
\item
  A: LF=2, LS=0
\end{itemize}

\textbf{Critical Path:} A → B → C → D → F → H (Float = 0 for all)
\textbf{Project Duration:} 20 weeks

\textbf{Non-critical Activity:} G has float of 3 weeks (LS=17, ES=14)

\subsection{Sample Problem}\label{sample-problem-9}

\textbf{Problem Statement:} A campus renovation project has the
following activities with crashing costs (cost to reduce duration by 1
week):

\begin{longtable}[]{@{}
  >{\raggedright\arraybackslash}p{(\linewidth - 10\tabcolsep) * \real{0.1351}}
  >{\raggedright\arraybackslash}p{(\linewidth - 10\tabcolsep) * \real{0.1757}}
  >{\raggedright\arraybackslash}p{(\linewidth - 10\tabcolsep) * \real{0.1622}}
  >{\raggedright\arraybackslash}p{(\linewidth - 10\tabcolsep) * \real{0.1757}}
  >{\raggedright\arraybackslash}p{(\linewidth - 10\tabcolsep) * \real{0.1622}}
  >{\raggedright\arraybackslash}p{(\linewidth - 10\tabcolsep) * \real{0.1892}}@{}}
\toprule\noalign{}
\begin{minipage}[b]{\linewidth}\raggedright
Activity
\end{minipage} & \begin{minipage}[b]{\linewidth}\raggedright
Normal Time
\end{minipage} & \begin{minipage}[b]{\linewidth}\raggedright
Crash Time
\end{minipage} & \begin{minipage}[b]{\linewidth}\raggedright
Normal Cost
\end{minipage} & \begin{minipage}[b]{\linewidth}\raggedright
Crash Cost
\end{minipage} & \begin{minipage}[b]{\linewidth}\raggedright
Predecessors
\end{minipage} \\
\midrule\noalign{}
\endhead
\bottomrule\noalign{}
\endlastfoot
A & 3 & 2 & \$1000 & \$1500 & - \\
B & 4 & 3 & \$2000 & \$2600 & A \\
C & 5 & 4 & \$1500 & \$2000 & A \\
D & 6 & 4 & \$3000 & \$4200 & B, C \\
\end{longtable}

Find the minimum cost schedule to complete in 10 weeks.

\textbf{Solution Approach:} Use CPM with time-cost trade-off analysis
(crashing). Calculate cost slope = (Crash Cost - Normal Cost)/(Normal
Time - Crash Time) and crash activities on critical path starting with
lowest cost slope.

\textbf{Python solution}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\ImportTok{import}\NormalTok{ networkx }\ImportTok{as}\NormalTok{ nx}
\ImportTok{import}\NormalTok{ pandas }\ImportTok{as}\NormalTok{ pd}
\ImportTok{from}\NormalTok{ itertools }\ImportTok{import}\NormalTok{ combinations}

\CommentTok{\# {-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}}
\CommentTok{\# 1. Setup Project Data}
\CommentTok{\# {-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}}
\CommentTok{\# Format: \{Activity: \{NormalT, CrashT, NormalC, CrashC, Predecessors\}\}}
\NormalTok{data }\OperatorTok{=}\NormalTok{ \{}
    \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}A\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: \{}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}nT\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: }\DecValTok{3}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}cT\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: }\DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}nC\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: }\DecValTok{1000}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}cC\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: }\DecValTok{1500}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}pred\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: []\},}
    \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}B\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: \{}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}nT\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: }\DecValTok{4}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}cT\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: }\DecValTok{3}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}nC\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: }\DecValTok{2000}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}cC\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: }\DecValTok{2600}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}pred\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: [}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}A\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{]\},}
    \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}C\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: \{}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}nT\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: }\DecValTok{5}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}cT\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: }\DecValTok{4}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}nC\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: }\DecValTok{1500}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}cC\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: }\DecValTok{2000}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}pred\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: [}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}A\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{]\},}
    \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}D\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: \{}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}nT\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: }\DecValTok{6}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}cT\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: }\DecValTok{4}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}nC\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: }\DecValTok{3000}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}cC\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: }\DecValTok{4200}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}pred\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: [}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}B\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}C\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{]\}}
\NormalTok{\}}

\NormalTok{TARGET\_DURATION }\OperatorTok{=} \DecValTok{10}

\CommentTok{\# {-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}}
\CommentTok{\# 2. Helper Functions}
\CommentTok{\# {-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}}

\KeywordTok{def}\NormalTok{ calculate\_slope(task):}
    \CommentTok{"""Calculates Cost Slope = (Crash Cost {-} Normal Cost) / (Normal Time {-} Crash Time)"""}
\NormalTok{    props }\OperatorTok{=}\NormalTok{ data[task]}
    \ControlFlowTok{if}\NormalTok{ props[}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}nT\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{] }\OperatorTok{==}\NormalTok{ props[}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}cT\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{]: }\ControlFlowTok{return} \BuiltInTok{float}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}inf\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{) }\CommentTok{\# Cannot crash}
    \ControlFlowTok{return}\NormalTok{ (props[}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}cC\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{] }\OperatorTok{{-}}\NormalTok{ props[}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}nC\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{]) }\OperatorTok{/}\NormalTok{ (props[}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}nT\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{] }\OperatorTok{{-}}\NormalTok{ props[}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}cT\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{])}

\KeywordTok{def}\NormalTok{ get\_project\_duration(current\_times):}
    \CommentTok{"""Runs CPM Forward Pass to find Project Duration"""}
\NormalTok{    G }\OperatorTok{=}\NormalTok{ nx.DiGraph()}
    \ControlFlowTok{for}\NormalTok{ task, props }\KeywordTok{in}\NormalTok{ data.items():}
\NormalTok{        G.add\_node(task, duration}\OperatorTok{=}\NormalTok{current\_times[task])}
        \ControlFlowTok{for}\NormalTok{ p }\KeywordTok{in}\NormalTok{ props[}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}pred\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{]:}
\NormalTok{            G.add\_edge(p, task)}
            
    \CommentTok{\# Calculate Earliest Finish (EF)}
\NormalTok{    early\_finish }\OperatorTok{=}\NormalTok{ \{\}}
    \ControlFlowTok{for}\NormalTok{ task }\KeywordTok{in}\NormalTok{ nx.topological\_sort(G):}
\NormalTok{        preds }\OperatorTok{=} \BuiltInTok{list}\NormalTok{(G.predecessors(task))}
\NormalTok{        es }\OperatorTok{=} \BuiltInTok{max}\NormalTok{((early\_finish[p] }\ControlFlowTok{for}\NormalTok{ p }\KeywordTok{in}\NormalTok{ preds), default}\OperatorTok{=}\DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{        early\_finish[task] }\OperatorTok{=}\NormalTok{ es }\OperatorTok{+}\NormalTok{ current\_times[task]}
        
    \ControlFlowTok{return} \BuiltInTok{max}\NormalTok{(early\_finish.values())}

\CommentTok{\# {-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}}
\CommentTok{\# 3. Initialization}
\CommentTok{\# {-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}}
\CommentTok{\# Current state of durations (Starts at Normal Time)}
\NormalTok{current\_times }\OperatorTok{=}\NormalTok{ \{t: data[t][}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}nT\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{] }\ControlFlowTok{for}\NormalTok{ t }\KeywordTok{in}\NormalTok{ data\}}
\NormalTok{current\_cost }\OperatorTok{=} \BuiltInTok{sum}\NormalTok{(data[t][}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}nC\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{] }\ControlFlowTok{for}\NormalTok{ t }\KeywordTok{in}\NormalTok{ data)}
\NormalTok{history }\OperatorTok{=}\NormalTok{ []}

\CommentTok{\# Calculate Slopes}
\NormalTok{slopes }\OperatorTok{=}\NormalTok{ \{t: calculate\_slope(t) }\ControlFlowTok{for}\NormalTok{ t }\KeywordTok{in}\NormalTok{ data\}}

\CommentTok{\# {-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}}
\CommentTok{\# 4. Iterative Crashing Loop}
\CommentTok{\# {-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}}
\BuiltInTok{print}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"{-}{-}{-} Crashing Analysis Log {-}{-}{-}"}\NormalTok{)}

\ControlFlowTok{while} \VariableTok{True}\NormalTok{:}
\NormalTok{    duration }\OperatorTok{=}\NormalTok{ get\_project\_duration(current\_times)}
\NormalTok{    history.append(\{}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}Duration\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: duration, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}Total Cost\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: current\_cost, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}Action\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{: }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}Status Check\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{\})}
    
    \ControlFlowTok{if}\NormalTok{ duration }\OperatorTok{\textless{}=}\NormalTok{ TARGET\_DURATION:}
        \BuiltInTok{print}\NormalTok{(}\SpecialStringTok{f"Target of }\SpecialCharTok{\{}\NormalTok{TARGET\_DURATION}\SpecialCharTok{\}}\SpecialStringTok{ weeks reached!"}\NormalTok{)}
        \ControlFlowTok{break}

    \CommentTok{\# Find the Best Move (Cheapest way to reduce duration by 1 unit)}
\NormalTok{    best\_move }\OperatorTok{=} \VariableTok{None}
\NormalTok{    min\_move\_cost }\OperatorTok{=} \BuiltInTok{float}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}inf\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{)}
    
    \CommentTok{\# Strategy A: Try crashing single tasks}
\NormalTok{    candidates }\OperatorTok{=}\NormalTok{ [t }\ControlFlowTok{for}\NormalTok{ t }\KeywordTok{in}\NormalTok{ data }\ControlFlowTok{if}\NormalTok{ current\_times[t] }\OperatorTok{\textgreater{}}\NormalTok{ data[t][}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}cT\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{]]}
    
    \ControlFlowTok{for}\NormalTok{ task }\KeywordTok{in}\NormalTok{ candidates:}
        \CommentTok{\# Simulate Crash}
\NormalTok{        current\_times[task] }\OperatorTok{{-}=} \DecValTok{1}
\NormalTok{        new\_dur }\OperatorTok{=}\NormalTok{ get\_project\_duration(current\_times)}
\NormalTok{        current\_times[task] }\OperatorTok{+=} \DecValTok{1} \CommentTok{\# Revert}
        
        \CommentTok{\# If duration decreased, this is a valid move}
        \ControlFlowTok{if}\NormalTok{ new\_dur }\OperatorTok{\textless{}}\NormalTok{ duration:}
\NormalTok{            cost }\OperatorTok{=}\NormalTok{ slopes[task]}
            \ControlFlowTok{if}\NormalTok{ cost }\OperatorTok{\textless{}}\NormalTok{ min\_move\_cost:}
\NormalTok{                min\_move\_cost }\OperatorTok{=}\NormalTok{ cost}
\NormalTok{                best\_move }\OperatorTok{=}\NormalTok{ [task]}

    \CommentTok{\# Strategy B: If single tasks fail (due to parallel critical paths), try pairs}
    \ControlFlowTok{if}\NormalTok{ best\_move }\KeywordTok{is} \VariableTok{None}\NormalTok{:}
        \CommentTok{\# We need to crash two parallel tasks simultaneously (e.g., B and C)}
        \ControlFlowTok{for}\NormalTok{ t1, t2 }\KeywordTok{in}\NormalTok{ combinations(candidates, }\DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{):}
\NormalTok{            current\_times[t1] }\OperatorTok{{-}=} \DecValTok{1}
\NormalTok{            current\_times[t2] }\OperatorTok{{-}=} \DecValTok{1}
\NormalTok{            new\_dur }\OperatorTok{=}\NormalTok{ get\_project\_duration(current\_times)}
\NormalTok{            current\_times[t1] }\OperatorTok{+=} \DecValTok{1}
\NormalTok{            current\_times[t2] }\OperatorTok{+=} \DecValTok{1}
            
            \ControlFlowTok{if}\NormalTok{ new\_dur }\OperatorTok{\textless{}}\NormalTok{ duration:}
\NormalTok{                cost }\OperatorTok{=}\NormalTok{ slopes[t1] }\OperatorTok{+}\NormalTok{ slopes[t2]}
                \ControlFlowTok{if}\NormalTok{ cost }\OperatorTok{\textless{}}\NormalTok{ min\_move\_cost:}
\NormalTok{                    min\_move\_cost }\OperatorTok{=}\NormalTok{ cost}
\NormalTok{                    best\_move }\OperatorTok{=}\NormalTok{ [t1, t2]}

    \CommentTok{\# Apply the Best Move}
    \ControlFlowTok{if}\NormalTok{ best\_move:}
\NormalTok{        actions\_str }\OperatorTok{=} \StringTok{""}
        \ControlFlowTok{for}\NormalTok{ task }\KeywordTok{in}\NormalTok{ best\_move:}
\NormalTok{            current\_times[task] }\OperatorTok{{-}=} \DecValTok{1}
\NormalTok{            current\_cost }\OperatorTok{+=}\NormalTok{ slopes[task]}
\NormalTok{            actions\_str }\OperatorTok{+=} \SpecialStringTok{f"Crash }\SpecialCharTok{\{}\NormalTok{task}\SpecialCharTok{\}}\SpecialStringTok{ ({-}1 wk, +$}\SpecialCharTok{\{}\NormalTok{slopes[task]}\SpecialCharTok{\}}\SpecialStringTok{); "}
        
        \BuiltInTok{print}\NormalTok{(}\SpecialStringTok{f"Current Duration: }\SpecialCharTok{\{}\NormalTok{duration}\SpecialCharTok{\}}\SpecialStringTok{ wks. Action: }\SpecialCharTok{\{}\NormalTok{actions\_str}\SpecialCharTok{\}}\SpecialStringTok{"}\NormalTok{)}
    \ControlFlowTok{else}\NormalTok{:}
        \BuiltInTok{print}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"Cannot crash further (Max limit reached)."}\NormalTok{)}
        \ControlFlowTok{break}

\CommentTok{\# {-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}}
\CommentTok{\# 5. Final Output}
\CommentTok{\# {-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}}
\NormalTok{df\_res }\OperatorTok{=}\NormalTok{ pd.DataFrame(history)}
\BuiltInTok{print}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"}\CharTok{\textbackslash{}n}\StringTok{{-}{-}{-} Final Project Schedule {-}{-}{-}"}\NormalTok{)}
\BuiltInTok{print}\NormalTok{(}\SpecialStringTok{f"Final Duration: }\SpecialCharTok{\{}\NormalTok{get\_project\_duration(current\_times)}\SpecialCharTok{\}}\SpecialStringTok{ weeks"}\NormalTok{)}
\BuiltInTok{print}\NormalTok{(}\SpecialStringTok{f"Final Total Cost: $}\SpecialCharTok{\{}\NormalTok{current\_cost}\SpecialCharTok{\}}\SpecialStringTok{"}\NormalTok{)}
\BuiltInTok{print}\NormalTok{(}\SpecialStringTok{f"Durations: }\SpecialCharTok{\{}\NormalTok{current\_times}\SpecialCharTok{\}}\SpecialStringTok{"}\NormalTok{)}
\BuiltInTok{print}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"{-}"} \OperatorTok{*} \DecValTok{40}\NormalTok{)}
\BuiltInTok{print}\NormalTok{(df\_res)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}
